<h2 class="majorMaps" id="textTitle"><strong>Archive - Critical requirements and Curriculum</strong></h2>

<p id="text">Below are the archives of program curricula. From 2007 through 2024, curriculum was presented via a major map - which outlined a major's critical requirements, courses, and optimal course sequence and aids students in remaining on track to graduation.</p>
<p id="text">The following list of programs is an archive of all undergraduate degrees that have been offered at Arizona State University since 2007. If a degree is no longer available, the last published description of the degree is provided.</p>
<p id="text">For the most up to date resources on current programs, please visit <a href="https://degrees.apps.asu.edu/bachelors">Degree Search</a>. </p>
<p id="text"><strong>Resources for students with a catalog year prior to 2007: 
</strong><br />
If a student's catalog year is prior to 2007-2008, the following resources are available for tracking degree requirements:<br />
<a 
	href="https://catalog.asu.edu/css"
	class="nodecor"
	title="Curriculum Check Sheets">Curriculum Check Sheets</a> <font class="divisor">&#160;|&#160;</font> <a
	href="https://webapp4.asu.edu/uachieve"
	class="nodecor"
	title="Degree Audit">Degree Audit</a> <font class="divisor">&#160;|&#160;</font> <a
	href=" https://catalog.asu.edu/catalog_archives"
	class="nodecor"
	title="General Catalog">General catalog</a> <font class="divisor">&#160;|&#160;</font> <a
	href="http://students.asu.edu/advising/directory"
	class="nodecor"
	title="Advisor Directory">Advisor directory</a><font class="divisor">&#160;|&#160;</font> <a
	href="https://catalog.apps.asu.edu/catalog/courses"
	class="nodecor"
	title="Course Catalog">Course catalog</a></p>

Our diverse majors include everything from biological sciences and business to psychology and sustainability.</p><p>Students pursuing an online bachelor’s degree also have the option to choose a minor
